The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. |\\\\\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. \"success\", \"notes\": \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\"}, \"plan005-sta-low-s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-13T22:20:47.812Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T06:31:05.119Z\", \"conclusion\": \"negative\", \"metrics\": {\"total_steps\": 500, \"duration_min\": 71, \"parse_rate\": 1, \"overall_prove_rate\": 0.5657, \"overall_isr\": 0.1682, \"last50_prove_rate\": 0.77, \"last50_isr\": 0.2559, \"last100_prove_rate\": 0.7725, \"last100_isr\": 0.2299, \"last100_loss\": 0.022752, \"overall_loss\": 0.017296, \"last100_isr_pr_ratio\": 0.298, \"last100_isr_lt_pr_count\": \"99/100\", \"first_rolling10_pr_ge_0.8\": 178, \"first_rolling10_pr_ge_0.9\": 439, \"max_rolling10_pr\": 0.9375, \"max_rolling10_isr\": 0.3282, \"windowed\": {\"1-50\": {\"pr\": 0.1775, \"isr\": 0.0906}, \"51-100\": {\"pr\": 0.1775, \"isr\": 0.0835}, \"101-200\": {\"pr\": 0.5062, \"isr\": 0.1463}, \"201-300\": {\"pr\": 0.6713, \"isr\": 0.1791}, \"301-400\": {\"pr\": 0.7013, \"isr\": 0.1984}, \"401-500\": {\"pr\": 0.7725, \"isr\": 0.2299}}}, \"notes\": \"conclusion=negative 基于 prove_rate 下降（last100 0.7725 vs exec-only 0.865）。但结构性发现：ISR/PR ratio=0.298，99/100 步 ISR &lt; 两个 eval 均已完成并更新 registry。汇总：

## Prolog exec-only 3-seed eval 结果 (eval_sta.py full STA)

| Seed | PR | Mean ISR | Proved-only ISR | n_proved |
|------|-----|----------|-----------------|----------|
| s42 | **83%** | 0.031 | 0.037 | 83 |
| s43 | **56%** | 0.008 | 0.015 | 56 |
| s44 | **21%** | 0.081 | 0.387 | 21 |
| **Mean±Std** | **53.3%±31.1%** | **0.040±0.037** | **0.146±0.210** | — |

关键发现：
- PR 跨 seed 极不稳定（std=31pp），与 FOL exec-only 一致
- Mean ISR 始终极低（<0.1），确认 Prolog exec-only 不产生 genuine reasoning traces（参考 C006: 低 ISR = 冗余编码，非无推理）
- s44 异常：PR 最低（21%）但 proved_only_ISR 最高（0.387, max=1.0），可能因少量 proved 样本中有几个碰巧 ISR 较高
